Deficit\",\n        \"Approval Missing\",\n        \"Total Errors\",\n        \"Error Summary\",\n    ]\n    headers_fmt = first8 + computed\n    ws_fmt.append(headers_fmt)\n\n    for e in entries:\n Code\")\n\n        try:\n            break_mins_num = 0 if break_mins is None else float(break_mins)\n        except Exception:\n            break_mins_num = 0\n        try:\n            min_break_num approval_missing)\n\n        if total_errors == 0:\n            error_summary = \"None\"\n        elif break_deficit and approval_missing:\n            error_summary = \"Break Deficit, Approval Missing\"\n        elif break_deficit:\n            error_summary = \"Break Deficit\"\n        else:\n            error_summary = \"Approval Missing\"\n\n        row_out = [e.get(h) for h in first8] + ID\", \"Week Ending\", \"Break Deficits\", \"Approval Gaps\", \"Total Errors\"])\n\n    agg = collections.OrderedDict()\n    for r in ws_fmt.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=True):\n        # Map grand_tot = 0\n\n    for emp, wk, bd, ag, tot in groups:\n        ws_sum.append([emp, wk, int(bd), int(ag), int(tot)])\n        grand_bd += int(bd)\n        grand_ag += int(ag)\n        grand_tot += int(tot)\n\n    ws_sum.append([\"Grand Total\", \"-\", grand_bd, grand_ag, grand_tot])\n\n    wb_out.save(out_xlsx_path)\n\n    return {\n        \"grand_break_deficits\": grand_bd,\n
